## AI Tools

### 001 · ChatGPT
* **Icon:** ![ChatGPT Icon](https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=chatgpt.com&sz=128)
* **Official Domain:** `chatgpt.com`
* **Category:** General-purpose AI assistants
* **Resource Type:** tool
* **Description:** Conversational AI assistant for writing, analysis, coding, image understanding, file work, and task assistance, available on web, mobile, and desktop, with API access offered separately through OpenAI.
* **Pricing & Free Classification:** freemium
* **Signup Required:** Yes
* **Local Execution / Self-Hostable:** No
* **Technical Tags:** `LLM`, `Chatbot`, `Multimodal`, `Productivity`, `API`
* **Verified Link:** [https://chatgpt.com](https://chatgpt.com/)
* **Canonical ID:** `tool:chatgpt`
* **Verification Status:** verified
* **Evidence Sources:** [chatgpt.com](https://chatgpt.com/), [GPT-5 Chat docs](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/models/gpt-5-chat-latest)
* **Notes:** ChatGPT is the consumer product; OpenAI API models are cataloged separately. GPT-5 Chat is documented as the non-reasoning GPT-5 snapshot used in ChatGPT.

## Batch Summary

| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| Record range | 001–050 |
| Tools / Models | 30 / 20 |
| Verified / Partially verified | 20 / 30 |
| Duplicates rejected | 0 |
| Next record number | 051 |

**Categories covered:** general-purpose assistants; coding assistants; search and research; image, video, audio, speech, and translation tools; productivity; automation; AI development platforms; RAG; vector databases; local inference; model serving; labeling; observability; LLMs; reasoning models; multimodal models; small language models; speech-to-text; image generation; segmentation.

**Still underrepresented:** MCP clients and servers; AI security and guardrails; robotics and embodied AI; evaluation and testing; customer support; marketing and SEO; music generation; OCR and document understanding; embedding and reranking models; video-generation models.

**Value legend:** pricing values used in this batch are `open_source`, `open_weights`, `freemium`, `usage_based`, and `paid`. `freemium` and `usage_based` are not in the original DevSpireHub enum (`completely_free | free_no_signup | free_tier | free_credits | free_local | open_source | open_weights | paid`). Map `freemium` → `free_tier` and `usage_based` → `paid` (or `free_tier` where the source notes a free allowance). The self-host value `Partial` is likewise outside the `Yes | No | Optional via Docker` set.

---

# Batch 2 - Records 051–100

Batch 2 prioritizes categories underrepresented in Batch 1: MCP infrastructure, guardrails, customer support, marketing and creative tools, robotics, OCR/document understanding, inference platforms, embeddings, reranking, and music models. All entries exclude the 50 canonical IDs accepted in Batch 1. [anthropic](https://www.anthropic.com/research/model-context-protocol)

## Batch Quality Summary

- **Accepted in this batch:** 50 unique records: 40 tools and 10 models.
- **Deduplication:** No overlap with Batch 1 canonical IDs, normalized names, official domains, or model identities. MCP protocol, Inspector, Registry, and individual servers are treated as distinct resources because each has an independent function.
- **Category correction:** The model share in this batch is lower because MCP, robotics, guardrails, OCR tooling, and inference platforms are primarily tools; subsequent batches should increase model coverage toward the 400-model target.
- **Verification status:** 23 records are `verified`; 27 are `partially_verified`.

# Batch 4 - Records 151–200

Batch 4 adds document-AI tooling, local chat and RAG platforms, audio workflows, and a model-heavy set covering speech, audio, multimodal, OCR, domain-specific, reranking, vision, image, and general-purpose models. All entries exclude the 150 canonical IDs already accepted. [docs.cohere](https://docs.cohere.com/docs/rerank)

Batch 5 continues increasing model coverage with speech, embedding, multimodal, code, and general-purpose models, while adding local inference and deployment tooling. All entries exclude the 200 canonical IDs already accepted. [legal.mistral](https://legal.mistral.ai/ai-governance/models/)

## Batch Quality Summary

- **Accepted in this batch:** 50 unique records: 10 tools and 40 models.
- **Cumulative distribution:** 115 tools and 135 models after five batches.
- **Deduplication:** No overlap with Batches 1–4. Closely related models were separated only where they have distinct architectures, capabilities, or release identities.
- **Verification status:** 24 records are `verified`; 26 are `partially_verified`.

# Batch 6 - Records 251–300

Batch 6 adds speech tooling, evaluation frameworks, and another model-heavy set covering TTS, speech recognition, video generation, domain models, embeddings, vision, and efficient language models. All entries exclude the 250 canonical IDs already accepted. [catalog.ngc.nvidia](https://catalog.ngc.nvidia.com/orgs/nvidia/nvigisdk/models/openvoice/-)

# Batch 8 - Records 351–400

Batch 8 adds speech, agent, and observability tooling, then emphasizes Wan and HunyuanVideo releases, speech models, embeddings, vision models, code models, and large general-purpose LLMs. All entries exclude the 299 canonical IDs already accepted. [docs.aws.amazon](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/us_en/bedrock/latest/userguide/model-card-qwen-qwen3-32b.md)

# Batch 9 - Records 401–450

Batch 9 adds speech-platform tooling and another model-heavy set covering Whisper sizes, DeepSeek releases, Qwen2.5 Coder sizes, Llama and Gemma variants, embeddings, vision models, and speech models. All entries exclude the 347 canonical IDs already accepted. [llama](https://www.llama.com/docs/model-cards-and-prompt-formats/llama3_2/)
